SELECT concert_Name, Year FROM singer_in_concert WHERE singer_ID = (SELECT MIN(Singer_ID) FROM singer WHERE Age = (SELECT MIN(Age) FROM singer))
SELECT DISTINCT c.Country FROM concert c JOIN singer_in_concert si ON cconcert_ID = siconcert_ID JOIN singer si ON si.singer_ID = si.singer_ID WHERE si.Age > 20
SELECT DISTINCT c.Country FROM singer s LEFT JOIN singer_in_concert c_on_concert ON s.Singer_ID = c_on_concert.Singer_ID WHERE s.Age > 20
SELECT c.Country FROM singer s JOIN concert c ON s.Singer_ID = c.Singer_ID WHERE c.Singer_ID IS NOT NULL GROUP BY s.Country COUNT(DISTINCT s.Singer_ID) AS Num_Singers ORDER BY Num_Singers DESC
SELECT concert_year, COUNT(DISTINCT "Country") AS country_count FROM singer GROUP BY concert_year, "Country"
WITH singer_average AS ( SELECT AVG(Age) AS average_age FROM singer ) SELECT Song_Name FROM singer_in_concert WHERE singer_in_concert.Singer_ID IN (SELECT Singer_ID FROM singer_average)
SELECT s.Song_Name FROM Singer s JOIN Concert c ON s.Singer_ID = c.Singer_ID JOIN Concert concert_ID ON s.Singer_ID = concert_ID WHERE s.Age > (SELECT AVG(Age) FROM Singer)
SELECT "Location", "Name" FROM "stadium" WHERE "Stadium_ID" = 5000 AND "Stadium_ID" = 10000 WITH BETWEEN 5000 AND 10000
SELECT "Location", "Name" FROM "stadium" WHERE "Capacity" BETWEEN 5000 AND 10000 AND "Capacity" AS INTEGER
SELECT MAX(Capacity) AS MaxCapacity, SUM(Capacity) / COUNT(*) AS AverageCapacity FROM Stadium
SELECT AVG(Capacity) as average_capacity, MAX(Capacity) as max_capacity FROM stadium
SELECT "Location", "Capacity" FROM "stadium" WHERE "Average" = (SELECT MAX("Average") FROM "stadium")
SELECT
SELECT COUNT(*) AS TotalConcerts FROM Concerts WHERE Year >= 2014 AND Year <= 2015
SELECT COUNT(*) FROM concert WHERE Year BETWEEN '2014' AND '2015'
SELECT
SELECT s.Stadium_ID, COUNT(cconcert_ID) AS "stadium_count" FROM concert JOIN concert ON concert.Stadium_ID = s.Stadium_ID GROUP BY s.Stadium_ID
SELECT Name, Capacity FROM concert GROUP BY Stadium_ID ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM concert WHERE Year >= 2014 AND Stadium_ID = Concert_ID) DESC HAVING (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM concert WHERE Year >= 2014 AND Stadium_ID = Concert_ID) >= 1
SELECT Name, Capacity FROM Stadium JOIN concerts ON Stadium_ID = concert_ID JOIN singer_in_concert ON concert_ID = singer_in_concertconcert_ID GROUP BY Name, Capacity ORDER BY SUM(concerts) DESC LIMIT 1
SELECT year, COUNT(*) AS ConcertCount FROM singer_in_concert GROUP BY year ORDER BY ConcertCount DESC LIMIT 1
SELECT Year FROM Concert GROUP BY Year HAVING COUNT(*) = (SELECT MAX(COUNT(*) FROM Concert) WHERE Year IS NOT NULL)
SELECT DISTINCT STadium.Location FROM Stadium WHERE NOT EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM singer_in_concert WHERE concert_ID = Stadium.Stadium_ID )
SELECT DISTINCT s.Name FROM Stadium s WHERE (SELECT DISTINCT singer_in_concert.Singer_ID FROM singer_in_concert WHERE concert_ID IN (SELECT Stadium.Stadium_ID FROM Stadium s)) = NULL
SELECT s.stadium_name FROM singer s JOIN concert c ON sconcert_ID = cconcert_ID JOIN stadium s ON c.stadium_ID = s.stadium_ID WHERE (SELECT MAX(singer_name) FROM singer s WHERE concert_ID = cconcert_ID AND singer_ID = s.Singer_ID) > 40 AND (SELECT MIN(singer_name) FROM singer s WHERE concert_ID = cconcert_ID AND singer_ID = s.Singer_ID) < 30 GROUP BY s.stadium_name HAVING COUNT(*) = 2
WITH Stadium_2014 AS ( SELECT DISTINCT "Stadium_ID" FROM "concert" WHERE "Year" = '2014' AND "Stadium_ID" NOT NULL ) SELECT "Location" FROM "stadium" WHERE "Stadium_ID" NOT IN (SELECT "Stadium_ID" FROM "Stadium_2014" )
SELECT DISTINCT s.Location FROM stadium s WHERE (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM concert c WHERE c.Year = 2014 AND c.Stadium_ID IN (SELECT Stadium_ID FROM concert c WHERE c.Year = 2014)) - (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM concert c WHERE c.Year = 2014 AND c.Stadium_ID IN (SELECT Stadium_ID FROM stadium s WHERE s.Stadium_ID IN (SELECT Stadium_ID FROM concert c WHERE c.Year = 2014))) = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM concert c WHERE c.Year = 2014) - (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM concert c WHERE c.Year = 2014 AND c.Stadium_ID IN (SELECT Stadium_ID FROM stadium s WHERE s.Stadium_ID IN (SELECT Stadium_ID FROM concert c WHERE c.Year = 2014))) GROUP BY s.Location ORDER BY s.Location
SELECT cconcert_ID, Name, Theme, COUNT(singer_ID) AS Number_of_Singers FROM concert c LEFT JOIN singer_in_concert sr ON cconcert_ID = srconcert_ID WHERE srconcert_ID IS NOT NULL
SELECT singer.Singer_ID, singer.Name, concert.Theme FROM singer LEFT JOIN singer_in_concert ON singer.Singer_ID = singer_in_concert.Singer_ID LEFT JOIN concert ON singer_in_concert.Stadium_ID = concert.Stadium_ID WHERE concert.Song_Name IS NULL

WITH enrolled_degree_programs AS ( SELECT student_id, COUNT(*) AS enrolled_degree_programs FROM Students JOIN Degree_Programs ON Students.student_id = Degree_Programs.student_id GROUP BY student_id HAVING COUNT(*) = 2 ), student_semester_enrollment AS ( SELECT student_id, first_name, middle_name, last_name, student_id FROM Students JOIN Semesters ON Students.student_id = Semesters.student_id ), enrolled_count AS ( SELECT enrolled_degree_programs FROM enrolled_degree_programs JOIN student_semester_enrollment ON enrolled_degree_programs.student_id = student_semester_enrollment.student_id ) SELECT s.first_name, s.middle_name, s.last_name, s.student_id FROM Students s JOIN enrolled_degree_programs e ON s.student_id = e.student_id JOIN student_semester_enrollment se ON s.student_id = se.student_id WHERE e.enrolled_degree_programs = 2

WITH ProgramStudents AS ( SELECT d.program_id, COUNT(DISTINCT s.student_id) AS student_count FROM Degree_Programs d LEFT JOIN Students s ON s.student_id = d.student_id LEFT JOIN Enrolment e ON e.student_id = s.student_id LEFT JOIN Enrolment_Courses es ON e.student_id = es.student_id LEFT JOIN Transcripts t ON e.transcript_id = t.transcript_id GROUP BY d.program_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT s.student_id) > 0 ) SELECT p.program_id, p.program_name, p.program_description, MAX(student_count) AS max_student_count FROM ProgramStudents GROUP BY p.program_id ORDER BY max_student_count DESC LIMIT 1
WITH StudentsPerDegree AS ( SELECT d-degree_summary_name, COUNT(s.student_id) AS total_students FROM Degree_Programs d JOIN Student_Enrolment s ON d.department_id = s.department_id GROUP BY d-degree_summary_name HAVING COUNT(s.student_id) > 0 ) SELECT d-degree_summary_name AS degree_summary_name FROM Degree_Programs d JOIN StudentsPerDegree sd ON d-degree_summary_name = sd_degree_summary_name GROUP BY d-degree_summary_name HAVING MAX(COUNT(s.student_id)) = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM StudentsPerDegree)
WITH StudentEnrolmentPrograms AS ( SELECT s.student_enrolment_id, p.program_id, p.summary FROM Student_Enrolment s JOIN Degree_Programs p ON sdegree_program_id = p.program_id GROUP BY s.student_enrolment_id, p.program_id HAVING COUNT(*) = 1 ORDER BY p.summary, s.student_enrolment_id LIMIT 1 ), Programs AS ( SELECT p.program_id, p.summary FROM Degree_Programs p WHERE s.department_id = s.department_id ORDER BY p.summary, p.program_id LIMIT 1 ) SELECT p.program_id, p.summary, s.name FROM StudentEnrolmentPrograms s JOIN Programs p ON s.program_id = p.program_id
SELECT d.department_id AS program_id, d.department_name AS degree_summary, COUNT(DISTINCT s.student_id) AS total_students FROM Degree_Programs d JOIN Student_Enrolment s ON d.department_id = s.degree_program_id GROUP BY d.department_id ORDER BY total_students DESC
SELECT s.student_id, s.first_name, s.middle_name, s.last_name, COUNT(c.course_id) AS enrollment_count, s.student_id FROM Students s JOIN Student_Enrolment e ON s.student_id = e.student_id JOIN Student_Enrolment_Courses c ON e.student_id = c.student_id JOIN Transcripts tr ON c.transcript_id = tr.transcript_id JOIN Transcript_Contents tc ON c.transcript_id = tc.student_course_id GROUP BY s.student_id ORDER BY COUNT(c.course_id) DESC LIMIT 1
with StudentNames as ( select s.first_name, s.middle_name, s.last_name, s.student_id, count(c.transcript_id) as enrolled_count from Students s join Student_Enrolment C on s.student_id = C.student_id join Student_Enrolment_Courses C on C.student_id = s.student_id group by s.first_name, s.middle_name, s.last_name, s.student_id having count(C.transcript_id) as enrolled_count ), MaxEnrolment as ( select max(enrolled_count) as max_enrolment from StudentNames ) select s.first_name, s.middle_name, s.last_name, s.student_id, max(enrolled_count) as enrollment_count, enrolled_count from Students s join Student_Enrolment C on s.student_id = C.student_id join Student_Enrolment_Courses C on C.student_id = s.student_id join MaxEnrolment m on m.student_id = s.student_id where m.max_enrolment = s.enrolled_count

SELECT c.course_name FROM Courses c JOIN Student_Enrolment_Courses s ON c.course_id = s.course_id GROUP BY c.course_name HAVING MAX(COUNT(s.course_id)) = COUNT(s.course_id)
WITH CourseEnrollmentCount AS ( SELECT c.course_name, COUNT(s.student_enrolment_id) AS enrolled_students FROM courses c JOIN student_Enrolment s ON c.course_id = s.course_id JOIN Student_Enrolment_Courses se ON s.student_course_id = se.student_course_id JOIN Transcripts tr ON tr.transcript_id = tr.transcript_id WHERE c.course_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY c.course_name HAVING COUNT(*) = 1 ORDER BY enrolled_students DESC LIMIT 1

as max_students FROM Student_Enrolment_Accessibility_Accessibility GROUP BY a.current_address_id ORDER BY max_students DESC LIMIT 1

SELECT c.Name, cPopulation FROM city c LEFT JOIN country cCountry ON c.CountryCode = cCountry.Code WHERE c.Language = 'English' HAVING cPopulation = (SELECT MAX(cPopulation) FROM city c WHERE c.Language = 'English' AND c.CountryCode = cCountry.Code)

WITH country_info AS ( SELECT c.Name AS country_name, cPopulation AS population, cHeadOfState AS head_of_state, cSurfaceArea AS area FROM country c LEFT JOIN city c_city ON c.CountryCode = c_city.CountryCode WHERE cSurfaceArea > 0 ORDER BY cSurfaceArea DESC ) SELECT country_name, population, head_of_state FROM country_info ORDER BY area DESC LIMIT 1
SELECT c.Name, COUNT(c.Language) AS LanguageCount FROM country c LEFT JOIN countrylanguage cl ON c.CountryCode = cl.CountryCode GROUP BY c.Name HAVING LanguageCount >= 3
SELECT c.Name, c.Code, COUNT(l.Language) AS Num_Languages FROM country c JOIN countrylanguage l ON c.Code = l.CountryCode GROUP BY c.Name, c.Code HAVING Num_Languages > 2
WITH avg_pop AS ( SELECT AVG(population) AS avg_pop FROM city WHERE population IS NOT NULL AND countryCode IS NOT NULL AND countryCode IS NOT NULL -- The above is redundant, but included for completeness ), city_pop AS ( SELECT district, population, countryCode FROM city WHERE population IS NOT NULL AND countryCode IS NOT NULL -- The above is redundant, but included for completeness ) SELECT district, COUNT(*) AS count_above_avg FROM city_pop LEFT JOIN avg_pop ON avg_pop.countryCode = city_pop.countryCode GROUP BY district ORDER BY district

with the most likes, you can use a SQL query that calculates the sum of likes for each student and then finds the student with the highest sum. SELECT name FROM Highschooler WHERE COUNT(liked_id) >= 2
SELECT name FROM Highschooler WHERE grade > 5 AND COUNT(DISTINCT friend_id) >= 2
SELECT name FROM Highschooler WHERE grade > 5 GROUP BY name HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT friend_id) >= 2
SELECT COUNT(liked_id) AS TotalLikes FROM Likes JOIN Highschooler ON (Likes.student_id = Highschooler.id AND Likes.liked_id = Highschooler.id)
SELECT COUNT(liked_id) FROM Likes LEFT JOIN Highschooler ON Likes.student_id = Highschooler.ID WHERE Highschooler.name = 'Kyle'
SELECT AVG(grade) FROM Highschooler WHERE EXISTS('Friend', 'student_id', 'highschooler_id')
SELECT s.student_id, AVG(l.grade) AS average_grade FROM Highschooler s LEFT JOIN Friend f ON s.student_id = f.student_id LEFT JOIN Likes l ON f.friend_id = l.liked_id WHERE f.student_id NOT NULL AND f.student_id IN (SELECT s.student_id FROM Highschooler s)

WITH FriendsAsStudents AS ( SELECT s.student_id, s.name, s_grade FROM Highschooler s LEFT JOIN Friend f ON s.student_id = f.student_id WHERE f.friend_id IS NULL GROUP BY s.student_id, s.name HAVING COUNT(f) = 0 WITHIN GROUP (SELECT s_grade FROM Highschooler s WHERE s.student_id = s.student_id) END ) SELECT MIN(average_grade) AS LowestGrade FROM FriendsAsStudents WHERE s.student_id IN (SELECT s.student_id FROM FriendsAsStudents)
SELECT s.state FROM owners o JOIN professionals p ON o.state = p.state GROUP BY s.state HAVING COUNT(o.name) > 0 AND COUNT(p.name) > 0 ORDER BY s.state ASC
SELECT state FROM owners LEFT JOIN professionals ON o.state = p.state GROUP BY state HAVING COUNT(o.id) > 0 AND COUNT(p.id) > 0 ON UPDATE SET owners.state = NULL, professionals.state = NULL FROM owners LEFT JOIN professionals ON o.state = p.state GROUP BY state HAVING COUNT(o.id) > 0 AND COUNT(p.id) > 0
SELECT AVG(dog.age) AS average_age FROM dogs JOIN professionals ON dogsprofessional_id = professionals.id WHERE (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM treatments WHERE treatment_id IN (SELECT treatment_id FROM treatments WHERE treatment_type_code = 'any' AND dog_id = d.id AND professional_id = p.id FOR ALL pProfessionalInTreatments) > 0 )
SELECT AVG(age) AS average_age FROM dogs JOIN treatments ON dogs.treatment_type_code = treatments.treatment_type_code
SELECT p.professional_id, p.first_name, p.last_name FROM professionals p WHERE p.state = 'IN' AND (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT t.treatment_id) AS treatment_count FROM treatments t WHERE t.professional_id = p.professional_id AND t.treatment_type_code = p.treatment_type_code ) > 2
SELECT professional_id, first_name, last_name, email_address FROM professionals WHERE state_code = 'IN' AND COUNT(treatment_id) > 2
SELECT d.name, t.treatment_amount FROM dogs d JOIN treatments t ON d.treatment_type_code = t.treatment_type_code WHERE t.treatment_amount <= 1000 GROUP BY d.dog_id ORDER BY t.treatment_amount DESC
WITH TreatmentsAs ( SELECT treatment_id, cost_of_treatment, dog_id FROM Treatments GROUP BY treatment_id, dog_id HAVING cost_of_treatment IS NOT NULL ORDER BY treatment_id, dog_id ) As DogsWithTreatments SELECT d.name FROM Dogs d JOIN TreatmentsAs t ON d.dog_id = t.dog_id FROM TreatmentsAs WHERE t.sum_cost <= 1000

SELECT owner_id, first_name, last_name, COALESCE(dog_id, 0) AS dog_count FROM Dogs GROUP BY owner_id HAVING SUM(COALESCE(dog_id, 0)) = COALESCE(dog_id, 0) ORDER BY dog_count DESC LIMIT 1
WITH DogOwnerCounts AS ( SELECT o.owner_id, COALESCE(DISTINCT D.dog_id, 0) AS dog_count FROM Owners o JOIN Dogs D ON o.owner_id = D.owner_id GROUP BY o.owner_id ORDER BY dog_count DESC TOP 1 ) SELECT o.owner_id, o.first_name, o.last_name FROM Owners o JOIN DogOwnerCounts CO ON o.owner_id = CO.owner_id ORDER BY CO.dog_count DESC LIMIT 1
SELECT professional_id, role, first_name FROM Professions LEFT JOIN Treatments ON Treatments.treatment_id = Professions.professional_id GROUP BY professional_id, role, first_name HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT treatment_id) >= 2
WITH TreatmentCount AS ( SELECT professional_id, COUNT(treatment_id) AS treatment_count FROM Treatments GROUP BY professional_id HAVING COUNT(treatment_id) >= 2 ) SELECT t.proessional_id, t.first_name, t.last_name FROM TreatmentCount
SELECT breed_name FROM dogs GROUP BY breed_code HAVING (SELECT MAX(count_of_dogs) AS max_count FROM ( SELECT count_of_dogs FROM dogs WHERE breed_code = breed_code ) AS count_of_dogs) WHERE count_of_dogs = max_count
SELECT breed_name, COUNT(*) AS DogCount FROM Dogs GROUP BY breed_name ORDER BY DogCount DESC LIMIT 1
SELECT o.owner_id, o.last_name FROM owners o JOIN treatments t ON t.dog_id = o.dog_id JOIN dogs d ON d.dog_id = t.dog_id GROUP BY o.owner_id UNION ALL SELECT o.owner_id, o.last_name FROM owners o JOIN treatments t ON t.dog_id = o.dog_id JOIN dogs d ON d.dog_id = t.dog_id JOIN treatments t ON t.dog_id = o.dog_id JOIN dogs d ON d.dog_id = t.dog_id JOIN treatments t ON t.dog_id = o.dog_id GROUP BY o.owner_id HAVING ( SELECT SUM(t.cost_of_treatment) AS total_treatments FROM treatments t JOIN dogs d ON d.dog_id = t.dog_id JOIN owners o ON o.owner_id = d.owner_id WHERE t.dog_id = o.dog_id ) AS total_treatments ORDER BY total_treatments DESC LIMIT 1
SELECT t.owner_id, t.last_name FROM Treatments t JOIN Dogs d ON t.dog_id = d.dog_id WHERE t.treatment_type_code = ( SELECT MAX(t2.cost_of_treatment) AS max_cost FROM Treatments t2 WHERE t2.dog_id = t.dog_id AND t.dog_id = d.dog_id )
SELECT t.treatment_type_description, b.breed_name, SUM(t.cost_of_treatment) AS total_cost FROM Treatments t JOIN Treatments t ON t.treatment_id = t.treatment_id JOIN Breeds b ON b.breed_code = t.treatment_type_code GROUP BY t.treatment_type_code ORDER BY SUM(t.cost_of_treatment) ASC LIMIT 1
SELECT description FROM Treatments WHERE cost_of_treatment = (SELECT MIN(cost_of_treatment) FROM Treatments)
with total_charges as ( select o.owner_id, o.zip_code, sum(treatment_cost) as total_charges from owners o join treatments t on t.treatment_id = o.treatment_id join dogs d on t.dog_id = d.dog_id group by o.owner_id, o.zip_code having sum(treatment_cost) = ( select sum(treatment_cost) from treatments t join dogs d on t.dog_id = d.dog_id join owners o on t.owner_id = o.owner_id group by t.treatment_id having count(d.dog_id) = (select count(*) from dogs where o.owner_id = o.owner_id) ) ) select o.owner_id, o.zip_code from owners o where o.owner_id in (select o.owner_id from total_charges) and o.zip_code in (select o.zip_code from total_charges) and total_charges.total_charges = ( select max(total_charges.total_charges) from total_charges )
SELECT o.owner_id, o.zip_code FROM Owners o JOIN Dogs d ON o.owner_id = d.owner_id JOIN Treatments t ON d.dog_id = t.dog_id GROUP BY o.owner_id ORDER BY ( SELECT SUM(t.cost_of_treatment) AS TotalCost FROM Treatments t JOIN Dogs d ON t.dog_id = d.dog_id JOIN Owners o ON d.owner_id = o.owner_id ) DESC LIMIT 1
